### Cutbolt for Claude Code · MIT

Editing recordings through reversible, saved agent operations and optional local speech recognition.
Exposes a local editing engine through MCP and six agent workflows for captioning, talking-head recuts, interviews, screen tutorials and delivery review. A configured AI agent decides and applies the edits.

Engine and skill versions must match. Background exports need Windows or Linux; a macOS build exists but is not a verification platform. The engine repo and this marketplace carry the same plugin, not two independent tools. Local media processing does not prevent external-agent data transmission.

### BiRefNet ONNX WebGPU conversion · MIT

Integrating learned background removal into a browser editor with a graph designed for WebGPU constraints.
Rewrites the BiRefNet ONNX graph for WebGPU background removal, addressing storage-buffer and unsupported-indexing limits while retaining the learned segmentation weights. Conversion and verification source scripts are included.
MIT source/model grant reviewed; training datasets retain separate terms, including the documented DIS5K commercial-use restriction on the dataset. Full-model downloads are about 1,038 MB fp32 or 520 MB fp16. Browser/GPU memory minima are not documented. The author tested Chrome 155 on an AMD RX 9070 XT with specific Transformers.js and ONNX Runtime Web versions; this does not establish every browser or device. Edge-mask differences and model-loading memory overhead are disclosed. Full setup and independent output checks remain pending.

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **OpenSuno-YuE2** · excluded: Bundled transcriber, MERT and core model source retains CC BY-NC 4.0. Root MIT covers original additions, not the complete integrated software. Existing historical record is preserved with a current failed license check. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)
- **FastGS** · needs-license-review: The existing license hold is confirmed by an Inria non-commercial/research/evaluation notice in core scene code. No full guide or recommendation is granted. [Source](https://github.com/fastgs/FastGS/blob/main/scene/__init__.py)
- **CollageRL** · excluded: The complete repository license is CC BY-NC 4.0, outside the open-source-only software scope. [Source](https://github.com/northadventure/CollageRL)
- **CollageNet and collage-diffusion-ui** · needs-license-review: Complete software-license retrieval returned 404 for both; public code and research demos are insufficient. [Source](https://github.com/joel-simon/CollageNet) [Source](https://github.com/linden-li/collage-diffusion-ui)
- **orbital-puppet** · needs-license-review: A concrete puppet project was found, but a complete software license was not retrieved. [Source](https://github.com/orbitalfoundation/orbital-puppet)
- **Black Latents diffusion app** · excluded: The application license itself is CC BY-NC-SA 4.0. Gated model access is an additional separate restriction. [Source](https://codeberg.org/martstilde/black-latents-latent-diffusion-app/src/branch/main/LICENSE)
- **AI Video Studio** · needs-license-review: Original code is MIT, but the notices list bundled GSAP under custom No Charge terms. Full distribution eligibility needs a dependency review before inclusion. [Source](https://github.com/tuila1freelancer/ai-video-studio/blob/main/NOTICE.md)
- **Prompt Canvas** · needs-license-review: The MIT plugin uses a separately licensed canvas SDK and external generation skill; its local generator is a mock fallback. The combined dependency terms need further review. [Source](https://github.com/lqshow/prompt-canvas)
- **sample-brain** · excluded: The reviewed quickstart uses rules. Its optional kNN path depends on an absent src/index.py embedding module, so an available creative AI workflow is not established. [Source](https://github.com/modus-mono/sample-brain/blob/main/src/classify.py)
- **Unavailable candidate repositories** · needs-license-review: BigSkyInteractive/touchfree-puppet-2d and prabhawati-x-labs/clipcraftAi metadata, README and license retrieval returned 404. They are not treated as verified projects. [Source](https://github.com/BigSkyInteractive/touchfree-puppet-2d) [Source](https://github.com/prabhawati-x-labs/clipcraftAi)
- **TouchDesigner AML operator family** · needs-license-review: The linked TRELLIS Core ML weights have a stated MIT grant, but the required operator repository is documentation/issues only and supplies no software license; the plugin is separately distributed to supporters. The model card does not establish an eligible open-source app. [Source](https://huggingface.co/mickeyvanolst/trellis-text-coreml) [Source](https://github.com/mickeyvanolst/TouchDesigner-AML-operator-family)
- **motionforge-ai preset engine** · excluded: The current index.html implements prompt keyword matching, CSS/canvas presets and WebM recording. No model inference or AI service call is present in the sole implementation, so the advertised AI generator is not eligible. [Source](https://github.com/rayanharis453-cyber/motionforge-ai/blob/main/index.html)
- **AI Procedural Texture Synthesis System** · needs-license-review: The repository contains a proposal and documents; its README lists the generator implementation as future work, and no complete software license was retrieved. It is not an available eligible creator tool. [Source](https://github.com/IdeaCrafter96/texture-synthesis-ai)
